[\[142a\]]
[\[142a.1\]]sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid brnyes pa/_bsgribs pa med pa thugs su chud pa rtogs pa/_phyir mi ldog pa'i chos dang ldan pa/_spyod yul la 'phrogs pa med pa/_dus gsum rnam par dgod pa bsam

[\[142a.3\]]mi mnga' ba/_/rnam par ma brtags pa'i sku dang ldan pa byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis yang dag par mnod pa'i ye shes can sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa mi gnyis pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog brnyes pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par thar pa tha 
[\[142a.4\]]mi dad pa'i ye shes kyi mthar phyin pa/_sangs rgyas kyis mtha' dang dbus med pa'i mnyam pa nyid thugs su chud pa/_chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/_nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa sangs rgyas kyi spyod pas rnam par spyod cing yul 
[\[142a.5\]]ma ga d+ha'i chos kyi dgon pa'i snying po'i byang chub la pho brang 'od kun nas byung ba na gdan seng ge'i khri pad mo'i snying po can la bzhugs shing /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa phrag bcu'i

[\[162b.6\]]chen po brdung bar bya'o/_/chos kyi sprin gyi char chen po mngon par dbab pa dang /_chos kyi sbyin pa chen po sbyin pa dang /_mi 'jigs pa chen po rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir/_seng ge'i nga ro chen pos rnam par spel ba'i smon lam chen pos chos

[\[173a.3\]]dang /_'dzam bu gling dang brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten gyi mgon po'i dbang phyug la dbang bar byed pa'i gzhal med khang dang /_bsam gtan dang rnam par thar ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang snyoms par 'jug pas shin tu rnam par rtse ba shes pa'i 
[\[173a.4\]]phyir byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la gnas pa'i dbang phyug la dbang bsgyur bas rnam par rtse ba'i mngon par shes pa'i gzhal med khang dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu'i rgyan la gnas pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rgyal srid dang dbang 
[\[173a.5\]]phyug bla na med pa la sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi dbang bsgyur ba yang dag par blang zhing chos thams cad la dbang sgyur ba'i rgyal srid gzung ba'i gzhal med khang ngo /_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi gzhal 
[\[173a.6\]]med khang ste/_de dag la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'gro ba thams cad du chos kyi dbang bskur bas/_rnam par 'phrul pa'i byin gyi rlabs bla na med pa thob par 'gyur ba dag go/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems 
[\[173a.7\]]dpa' rnams kyi dga' ba ste/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di ltar 'khrug pa med pas dran pa'i dga' ba dang /_rab tu rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas pas blo gros kyi dga' ba dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung nas mngon sum du chos thos pa 
[\[173b\]]
[\[173b.1\]]yongs su zad par mi 'gyur bas rtogs pa'i dga' ba dang /_phyogs bcu'i tshad med par khyab pas blo'i dga' ba dang /_sems can gyi lus sgo mtha' yas pa yongs su ston pas byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par 'phrul pa'i dga' ba dang /_ting nge 
[\[173b.2\]]'dzin gyi sgo gcig gis ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo thams cad la 'jug pas ting nge 'dzin gyi dga' ba dang /_thos pa thams cad 'dzin pa nyams pa med par kun tu 'dzin cing sems can gzhan gyi rgyud kyis 'thob par byed pas gzungs kyi dga' ba dang /_tshig gcig 
[\[173b.3\]]dang yi ge gcig gis bskal pa brjod du med par gzhan dang gzhan du smra zhing nges pa'i tshig rgyud mi 'chad par rab tu ston pas spobs pa'i dga' ba dang /_sems can thams cad kyi lus la mtha' yas pa'i sgo'i rgyud du mngon par byang chub pa ston pas mngon par 
[\[173b.4\]]rdzogs par byang chub pa'i dga' ba dang /_bdud dang phyir rgol ba thams cad chos dang 'thun par tshar gcod pas chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i dga' ba'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dga' ba ste/_de 
[\[173b.5\]]dag la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' rnams sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi dgyes pa bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rgyan te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di ltar thub pa med 
[\[173b.6\]]pas stobs kyi rgyan dang /_zil gyis mi non pas mi 'jigs pa'i rgyan dang /_don brjod du med cing yangs pa brjod pa'i sgo rgyun mi 'chad pas don gyi rgyan dang /_chos kyi phung po stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhi shin tu brtags pa'i chos rab tu ston pa la mgo 
[\[173b.7\]]rmongs pa med pas chos kyi rgyan dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis bya ba'i smon lam las phyir mi ldog pas smon lam gyi rgyan dang /_kun tu bzang po'i theg pas 'byung bas spyod pa'i rgyan dang /_zhing thams cad 
[\[174a\]]
[\[174a.1\]]zhing gcig du byin gyis rlob pas zhing gi rgyan dang /_zhing thams cad du chos kyi sprin chen po'i char rgyas par rab tu 'bebs pas/_dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan dang bskal ba grangs med pa thams cad du spyod pa'i

[\[174b.5\]]zhe na 'di ltar sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar yongs su mi gtong ba dang /_sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /_rnam par gdul ba yongs su mi gtong ba dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi

[\[188b.3\]]sems bskyed pa ste/_bcu gang zhe na/_kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la chos thams cad mtha' yas pa yin yang byang chub sems dpa' ni 'di ltar chos thams cad dus gsum ma lus par shes pas mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar bya'o/_/snyam 
[\[188b.4\]]du sems bskyed pa 'di ni theg pa chen po'i go ca rdo rje lta bu'i sems bskyed pa dang po'o/_kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang skra'i rtse mo'i go dang /_sgo re re la yang sems can mtha' yas te mu med pas/_chos kyi dbyings thams cad kyi sems can lta 
[\[188b.5\]]smos kyang ci dgos na byang chub sems dpa' ni go ca chen po bgos shing 'di skad du sems can thams cad mya ngan las 'das pa chen po bla na med pas/_yongs su mya ngan las bzla bar bya'o/_/zhes bya ba'i tshig brjod pa 'di ni theg pa chen po'i go ca rdo rje 
[\[188b.6\]]lta bu'i sems bskyed pa gnyis pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'jig rten gyi khams rnams ni mtha' yas mu med pa ste/_ris med las mtshams med pa na byang chub sems dpa' ni 'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya ba bla 
[\[188b.7\]]na med pas 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad yongs su sbyang bar bya'o/_/zhes smon lam mngon par sgrub cing de yang yang dag pa dang /_dben par yongs su sbyor ba 'di ni theg pa chen po'i go ca rdo rje lta bu'i sems bskyed pa gsum pa'o/_/kye 
[\[189a\]]
[\[189a.1\]]rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'gro ba thams cad ni mtha' yas mu med pa ste/_ris med la sa mtshams med pa la byang chub sems dpa' ni 'di ltar 'gro ba thams cad bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi snang ba chen pos snang bar bya'o

[\[189a.5\]]la yang mngon par chags par mi byed de de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la yang mngon par chags par mi byed cing /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku de dag kyang yod par yang mi byed/_med par yang mi byed med pa ma yin par yang mi byed/_yod pa ma yin

[\[189b.1\]]sems bskyed pa drug pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems can gzhan dang gang zag gzhan dag gi mgo bcad par gyur tam/_lag pa bcad dam/_rkang pa bcad dam/_rna ba dang sna bcad dam/_mig phyung bar

[\[209a\]]
[\[209a.1\]]sems dpa' rnams kyi chos ma 'dres pa brgyad pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems brtan pa dang ldan pa'i lhag pa'i bsam pa'i go ca shin tu bgos pas/_de dag ji ltar nyon mongs shing byis 
[\[209a.2\]]pa'i skye bo'i 'du 'dzi dang /_ji ltar bsten tam/_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi tshogs kyi nang du ji ltar phyin kyang rung ste/_thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems rin po che bzang po 'od dang ldan pa de 'jig par mi byed do/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras de ni 'di lta ste 
[\[209a.3\]]dper na nor bu rin po che chen po rgyan bzang po zhes bya ba de chu dang bar byed pa ni pad mo'i rdzing bu ji ltar rnyog kyang rung ste/_nang du bcug na nor bu rin po che'i 'od gsal zhing bzang po de nyid rnam par mi spong ste/_chu de thams cad kyang rnyog pa med par rab tu dang bar byed do/_/kye 
[\[209a.4\]]rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang byis pa'i skye bo'i 'du 'dzis ji ltar bsten kyang thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems rin po che'i 'od gsal zhing snang ba bzang po de chud zos par mi byed de sems can de dag kyang nyon mongs pa'i rnyog pa de las zlog cing thams cad 
[\[209a.5\]]mkhyen pa'i sems rin po che'i 'od gsal zhing snang ba de nyid la rab tu 'god do/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar ci ltar nyon mongs par gyur kyang rung /_byis pa'i skye bo'i 'du 'dzi dang /_ji ltar bsten tam/_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi tshogs kyi nang du ji ltar 
[\[209a.6\]]phyin kyang rung ste/_thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems rin po che de nyid 'jig par mi byed pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa ste/_'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa dgu pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni rang gi yul gyi 
[\[209a.7\]]ye shes kyi chos la dbang bsgyur ba'i pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa thob pa ste/_rang byung gi ye shes kyi dbang bskur bas dbang bskur zhing go 'phang skyon med pa la gnas kyang dge ba'i bshes gnyen la gnas pa ring du mi spong zhing de bzhin gshegs pa la rim gro bya ba'i 
[\[209b\]]
[\[209b.1\]]rgyun mi gcod de/_de ltar tshul phun sum tshogs pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad du dge ba'i bshes gnyen bsten pa las phyir mi ldog cing de bzhin gshegs pa la rim gro bya ba rgyun mi gcod pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa ste/_'di ni byang chub 
[\[209b.2\]]sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa bcu pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos ma 'dres pa ste/_de dag la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos ma 'dres pa bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go_/
[\[209b.3\]]kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi las te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad yongs su sbyang ba'i las dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad bsnyen bkur ba'i las dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i grogs thams 
[\[209b.4\]]cad dang dge ba'i rtsa ba gcig tu bya ba'i las dang /_sems can gyi khams thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba'i las dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i dus thams cad kyi phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug bar yongs su gzung ba'i las dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'gro yang 'jig 
[\[209b.5\]]rten gyi khams gcig nas mi g.yo ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyi las dang /_kha dog mtha' yas pa'i 'od gzer dgye ba'i 'od kyi las dang /_pad mo'i snying po la byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs 'khod pa yongs su bstan pa'i 'od gzer gyi las dang /_mya ngan las 'das 
[\[209b.6\]]pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bstan pa gzung bas dkon mchog gsum gyi rigs rgyun mi gcad pa'i las dang /_'jig rten thams cad du rgyu zhing chos bstan pa dang /_sems can gdul ba yang dag par bsgrub pa'i sprul pa'i las dang /_nam 
[\[209b.7\]]'dod pa dang nam sems bskyed pa na smon lam yongs su rdzogs par sems can la yang dag par ston pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi las so/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi las te/_de dag la 
[\[210a\]]
[\[210a.1\]]gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'phrin las rgya chen po bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lus te/_bcu gang zhe na 'di

[\[210a.5\]]chub sems dpa'i lus ni mtshan bzang po dang ldan pa'o/_/chos kyi mtshan nyid med pa'i mthar thug pas byang chub sems dpa'i lus ni mtshan nyid med pa'o/_/'das pa dang /_ma byon pa dang /_da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 
[\[210a.6\]]sku dang mnyam pa nyid thob pas byang chub sems dpa'i lus ni kun tu song ba'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lus te/_de dag la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku mi zad pa bla na

[\[210b\]]
[\[210b.1\]]can gyi khams thams cad du sgo yongs su ston pa'i lus kyi las dang /_skye ba thams cad du gnas pa'i lus kyi las dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'gro ba'i lus kyi las dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 'gro ba'i lus kyi 
[\[210b.2\]]las dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad lag pa gcig gis kun tu khebs par byed pa'i lus kyi las dang /_khor yug dang /_rdo rje'i ri thams cad lag pa gcig gis nyug pa'i lus kyi las dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rang gi lus 'jig pa

[\[210b.5\]]dag go/_kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rang bzhin te/_bcu gang zhe na 'di ltar nan tan dang ldan pas pha rol tu phyin pa'i rang bzhin dang /_'gro ba thams cad yongs su mi gtong bas/_bsdu ba'i dngos po'i rang bzhin dang /_

[\[210b.7\]]kyi rang bzhin dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku dang /_gcig tu 'dres pas ye shes kyi rang bzhin dang /_skye ba'i skye mched thams cad las 'das pas chos kyi rang bzhin dang /_thams cad du sgo yongs su ston pas thams cad kyi rang bzhin dang /_rdzu 'phrul

[\[219a.5\]]dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i chos nges pa 'thob pa ste/_bcu gang zhe na 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skyes pa yin/_sangs rgyas kyi yul la gnas pa yin/_byang chub sems dpa'i las la zhugs pa 
[\[219a.6\]]yin/_pha rol tu phyin pa la nan tan du byed pa yin/_sangs rgyas kyi rgyud na sa byung ba yin/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung las skyes pa yin/_sangs rgyas kyi stobs la gnas pa yin/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i byang chub tu rjes su song ba yin/_
[\[219a.7\]]sangs rgyas thams cad dang sku dang rang bzhin gcig pa yin/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rnam par spyod pa dang mi gnyis pa yin/_kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i chos nges par 'thob pa 
[\[219b\]]
[\[219b.1\]]ste/_de dag ni skyon med pa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 'thob pa dag go/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam ste/_bcu gang zhe na 'di ltar dge ba'i bshes gnyen 
[\[219b.2\]]rnams dang rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu 'gyur ba dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba thun mong du mos pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam mo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul pa dang /_tshul mthun zhing smon lam chen po la dbugs phyin pa thob pa 
[\[219b.3\]]ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam mo/_/sems rgya chen pos rang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i go 'byed pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam mo/_/las ji ltar byas pa chud mi za bas bskal pa 'grangs pa thams cad du mi skyo ba ni/_sangs rgyas kyi chos

[\[219b.5\]]smin par bya ba la mkhas pas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgyun mi gcod pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam mo/_/snying rje chen po bskyed de sems tshad med par byed pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam mo/_/nam mkha'i dbyings thams

[\[220a\]]
[\[220a.1\]]sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'byung ba'i lam ste/_de dag gis byung ba'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi skyes bu dam pa'i ming bcu 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di

[\[220a.3\]]chos kyi dam pa mngon par rtogs pas dam pa'i sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_mchog gi chos mngon par 'tshang rgya bas mchog gi sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_ye shes kyi khyad par rab dang ldan pas rab kyi sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_phul du brtson 
[\[220a.4\]]'grus rtsom pas phul gyi sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_gong na med pa'i chos yang dag par ston pas bla na med pa'i sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_stobs bcu'i ye shes yangs pa dang ldan pas stobs kyi sems dpa' zhes bya ba yin/_'gro ba

[\[220a.7\]]bas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam gcig la rten pa dang /_thabs dang shes rab kyis mngon par bsgrub pas/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam yan lag gnyis pa dang /_stong ba nyid kyi sbyor ba dang /_mtshan ma med pa'i mtha'i rtse 
[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]mor phyin pa dang /_smon pa med pa'i phyir khams gsum dang mi 'dre bas byang chub sems dpa'i lam rnam pa gsum pa dang /_nyes pa bshags pa'i tshul dang /_bsod nams thams cad la rjes su yi rang ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa la gsol ba 
[\[220b.2\]]gdab pa'i rim gro rgyun mi gcod pa dang /_yongs su bsngo ba la mkhas pas shin tu bsngo ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i lam spyod pa bzhis smos pa dang dad pa'i rtsa ba tshugs te/_shin tu brtan pa dang /_brtson 'grus chen po las phyir mi ldog pa dang /_
[\[220b.3\]]dran pa rtse gcig pa dang ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul ma 'khrugs pa dang /_rab tu rtogs pa'i shes rab kyi spyod yul la mkhas pas/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam dbang po lngas rnam par spel ba dang /_'di lta ste lha'i mig gis 'jig rten gyi khams 
[\[220b.4\]]thams cad kyi gzugs kyi rnam pa thams cad dang /_sems can gyi shi 'pho dang /_skye ba thams cad yongs su mthong ba'i rang bzhin dang ldan pa dang /_lha'i rna bas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos bstan pa mnyan cing dran par bya ba dang /_

[\[220b.6\]]bskal pa 'grangs pa thams cad rjes su dran zhing dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par 'phel bar byed pa dang /_rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul pa chen po bsgrubs pas sems can ji ltar 'dul ba bzhin du cho 'phrul sna tshogs yongs su ston cing /_sems can thams cad la chos 
[\[220b.7\]]brjod pa dang /_zag pa zad pa'i ye shes dang ldan pas yang dag pa'i mtha' mngon sum du mthong zhing byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bsgrub pa rgyun mi gcod pas/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam mngon par shes pa drug gis rnam par 'phrul ba dang /_
[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]'di lta ste/_sems can gyi gtso bo mtha' yas pa thams cad skra'i rtse mo gcig la bzhugs par mthong ste/_yang dag par yongs su gzung zhing sems can gyi bsam pas rnam par rig par bya ba la 'jug pas sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa dang /_
[\[221a.2\]]de bzhin gshegs pa gcig gi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor nas ma g.yos par de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du mngon du zhal nas chos bstan pa bsgrags pa mi zad pas 'gro ba thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /_dbang po ji lta ba bzhin du 
[\[221a.3\]]rnam par rig par bya ba la 'jug pas chos rjes su dran pa dang /_phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs mthong ba dang mi 'bral zhing 'gro ba thams cad la byang chub sems dpa'i lus yongs su ston pas dge 'dun rjes su dran pa dang /_byang chub sems 
[\[221a.4\]]dpa' thams cad kyi sbyin pa yongs su gtong ba la yang dag par gzhol zhing gtong ba chen pos rnam par spel ba yid la byed pas gtong ba rjes su dran pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems dang mi 'bral bas dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad 'gro ba thams cad la stobs pas/_tshul 
[\[221a.5\]]khrims rjes su dran pa dang /_dga' ldan gyi pho brang gi mchog na byang chub sems dpa' tshe gcig gis thogs pa bzhugs pa rjes su dran pas lha rjes su dran pa dang /_thabs kyi ye shes chen pos 'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /_gdul ba rgyun 
[\[221a.6\]]mi 'chad pa la zhugs te/_sems can rjes su dran pa'i phyir dge 'dun rjes su dran pas byang chub sems dpa'i lam rjes su dran pa bdun gyis mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /_'di lta ste log par lta ba thams cad yongs su spangs pas yang dag pa'i lta ba'i lam du 
[\[221a.7\]]'byung ba dang /_rtog pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa dang /_yongs su rtogs pa dang bral bas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang mthun zhing don yod la yid la bya ba med pas byung ba dang /_ngag gi nyes pa bzhi dang bral bas 'phags pa dang mthun 
[\[221b\]]
[\[221b.1\]]zhing yang dag pa'i ngag rab tu brjod pa dang /_lus kyi las thams cad sems can nye bar 'tsho bas 'gro ba thams cad thogs pa med par yongs su smin par byed cing 'dul bas mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /_'phags pa'i rigs la rab tu gnas shing sbyangs 
[\[221b.2\]]pa'i yon tan gyis sdom pa byang chub kyi lam dang ldan pa'i spyod lam dang /_spyod pa dang tshul la rab tu gnas shing thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med pas yang dag pa'i 'tsho ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi brtul zhugs dang /_dka' thub kyis 
[\[221b.3\]]kun nas gdungs pa dang /_yongs su gdungs pa yang dag par ston cing /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu'i rang bzhin thogs pa med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir yang dag pa'i rtsol ba dang /_sgra thams cad 'dzin cing dran pa 'jig rten gyi rnam par 'khrug 
[\[221b.4\]]pa thams cad kyis bar du mi chod pa'i phyir yang dag pa'i dran pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i ting nge 'dzin gcig gis ting nge 'dzin thams cad mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa'i phyir yang dag pa'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[221b.5\]]dang ldan pas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam ni 'phags pa'i lam gyi yan lag brgyad dang ldan zhing byang chub dang mthun par gyur pa dang /_'di lta ste 'dod pa dang gnod sems dang /_'tsho ba med pa dang ngag gi las thams cad kyis ma bsgribs 
[\[221b.6\]]par chos ston pa'i tshul dang ldan pas rnam par rtog pa dang /_rnam par dpyod pa gsal ba dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i bsam pa yongs su mi gtong zhing dga' ba zing zing po dang bral ba dang /_

[\[222a\]]
[\[222a.1\]]med pa'i snyoms par zhugs pas mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /_'dod pa'i khams dang gzugs kyi khams su skyes pa las ma g.yos par 'du shes pa dang /_tshor ba thams cad 'gog pa'i snyoms par 'jug pas mngon par bsgrubs

[\[222b.1\]]pa thams cad skad cig gcig du gzung ba rtogs pa'i ye shes snang bas mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa dang bag chags dang bsam pa dang /_bag la nyal ba byang bar thob pa'i ye shes kyi tshul gyis mngon par bsgrubs pa'i byang chub

[\[224a\]]
[\[224a.1\]]pa la mkhas pa bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lam gyi rgyan te bcu gang zhe na/_kye rgyal ba'i sras/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa'i khams

[\[234a.1\]]tu dgod pa'i phyir sems can sems rnam par 'khrug pa can la rnam par lta ba dang /_the tsom med pas blta ba dang /_dmigs pa thams cad rab tu bstsal ba'i phyir sems can shes rab 'chal ba can la rnam par lta ba dang /_ji ltar bstan pa'i

[\[234a.6\]]khur bkur ba'i phyir glang po'i bsgyings pa dang /_chos kyi sprin chen po shin tu 'dus pa las rig pa dang /_rnam par grol ba'i glog 'gyu zhing yang dag pa ma nor bar 'brug sgra chen po bsgrags te/_dbang po dang stobs dang byang chub kyi yan lag dang bsam gtan dang /_
[\[234a.7\]]rnam par thar ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa'i bdud rtsi'i chu'i chos kyi char mngon par dbab pa'i phyir klu'i rnam par bsgyings pa dang /_ma rig pa'i sgo nga'i sbubs dang /_lcag lcig gi spri brtol te/_sred pa'i chu lhag mthong gi gshog

[\[234b.1\]]pas phye ste/_sems kyi sprul 'dod pa dang /_'khrug pa dang /_nyon mongs pa'i rnyog pa can mngon par blang zhing 'byung ba'i phyir/_nam mkha' lding gi dbang po chen po'i rnam par bsgyings pa dang /_'jigs pa dang bag tsha ba thams cad med cing ye shes chen po mnyam pa

[\[253b.3\]]dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dga' ldan na gnas pa'i las te/_de dag byas nas byang chub sems dpa' rnams phyis mi'i 'jig rten du 'ong bar 'gyur ba dag go_/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dga' ldan gyi gnas nas shi 'pho ston 
[\[253b.4\]]pa ste/_bcu gang zhe na/_kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la byang chub sems dpa' rnams lha'i ris dga' ldan nas shi 'pho ba'i tshe/_rkang pa'i mthil gyi 'og nas bde ba'i rgyan ces bya ba'i 'od zer 'byung ste/_de byung nas kyang stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams

[\[253b.6\]]'gyur ro/_bde ba'i nyams ni nyams su tshor ma thag tu de dag gis 'di ltar grogs po dag sems can gzhan zhig ni 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir skyes so zhes bya bar shes te/_'di ni dga' ldan gyi gnas nas shi 'pho ston pa dang po'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang

[\[267a.2\]]snang byed chos kyi nyi ma'i khyu mchog 'gro ba kun tu 'byung /_/chos dbyings rnam par dag pa dkyil 'khor 'gro ba kun gyis blta na mdzes pa ste/_/nyan thos rang rgyal rnams kyi tshogs mang skar ma 'dra ba dag ni zil gyis mnan/_/sems kyi rgyud rnams mthong nas srid pa gsum 
[\[267a.3\]]du 'phel dang 'grib pa rnam par ston/_/de dang de dag ye shes zla ba 'gro la 'char zhing mkhas pa nam mkha'i mtha'/_/chos kyi dbang po 'gro ba kun la dbang byed gzugs dang yon tan dag gis brgyan/_/ye shes rdo rje mnga' ba thabs ni [{]myig [,]mig [}]ste chos la dbang chen bsgyur 
[\[267a.4\]]gnas pa/_/lus dang mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po sna tshogs kun gyi blta ba'i dbang po ste/_/chos kyi 'gro ba kun las rgyal ba de dang de dag chos kyi dbang po yin/_/srid pa gsum na 'dod pa'i 'dam dang nyon mongs las las rab 'das kun dbang po/_/byams pa 
[\[267a.5\]]snang dang thugs rje bsam gtan dga' dang btang snyoms rnams la mos/_/srid pa gsum po kun tu tha dad lus ston chos kyi sgras kyang go byed pa/_/de dang de dag tshangs pa lta bur rnam dag mos shing lta ba'i rnam pa sel/_/'khor las rab tu 'das shing rnam dag 
[\[267a.6\]]yul dang ldan pa bdag po chos kyi dbang /_/phyir ldog [{]myi [,]mi [}]'gyur blo gros che 'chang gang rnams theg pa dma' dang tha dad la/_/chos kyi dbang por dbang bskur 'jig rten dam pa ye shes phyir ldog [{]myi [,]mi [}]'gyur ba/_/thams cad mkhyen pa'i yon tan dbang phyug grags chen

[\[271b.1\]]bsam pa thob pas ngo mtshar 'du shes gnas pa rnams/_/spyod pa mchog dang ldan zhing byams pa'i rjes song brtson 'grus brtan pas rnam gnon cing /_/tshul khrims [{]myi [,]mi [}]g.yo dbugs phyin de dag 'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par byed/_/lung bstan rab tu thob 
[\[271b.2\]]nas rgyal sras rnams kyi spyod pa tshul bzhin thob par byed/_/sangs rgyas yon tan dag la zhugs kyang sems can sems dang zhing la sems pa byed/_/bskal pa'i dus la 'jug pa'i ye shes ldan zhing mkhas pa skyo ba'i 'du shes [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/gzungs kyi tshul 
[\[271b.3\]]dang yang dag tha dad stobs rnams thob cing yang dag don gyis rgal/_/bstan pa mtshungs [{]myed [,]med [}]sems kyis mkhas pa byang chub zhi ba rnam par 'tshang yang rgya/_/sems kyi spyod pa thams cad bzang po'i yon tan spyod pa'i mchog tu smon par byed/_/snying rje'i rkyen dang 
[\[271b.4\]]byams pa mchog sten byang chub dam pa spyod par byed dang ldan/_/brtson pas pha rol phyin rtogs rab tu mkhas shing skyo ba'i pha rol phyin gyur pas/_/khyu mchog mos dang rab tu rtogs pa'i stobs thob byang chub dam pa mngon 'tshang rgya/_/mnyam pa nyid kyis mnyam pa thob 
[\[271b.5\]]cing ye shes tshul gyis chos mchog ston byed pa/_/yon tan thob byed spobs dang ldan pa de dag chos kyi dbang phyug mchog tu 'gyur/_/dbang la gnas kyang gnas pa [{]myed [,]med [}]pas mnyam pa [{]myed [,]med [}]pa'i sems kyi 'brug sgra sgrog_/ye shes mchog dang de bzhin sprul 
[\[271b.6\]]pa sgrub cing byang chub nye bar 'gro/_/mkhas pa de dag bskal pa 'byam klas byin rlabs shes pas dbugs phyin rab tu 'thob/_/zab la gnas pas brtan par 'gro na gnas shing nem nur bstsal bas mkhas pa rnams/_ye shes bsam gyis mi 
[\[271b.7\]]khyab chos kyi dgongs pa mkhas shing rnam phye pha rol phyin/_/snyoms par 'jug pa'i ye shes yul la zhugs nas thams cad mkhyen par 'tshang yang rgya/_/ye shes rnam thar kun gyi pha rol phyin cing mngon par shes pas rnams par rtsen/_/rig pa'i snang 
[\[272a\]]
[\[272a.1\]]ldan rnam grol nyon mongs bral bas 'gro la skyed mos tshal dang 'dra/_/gang la dkar ba'i spyod pa'i gzhal [{]myed [,]med [}]khang mdzes sna tshogs spyod pa ston pa po/_/'gro ba kun la rgyan rnams du ma rab tu ston kyang sems las g.yo ba [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/
[\[272a.2\]]bsam pa shin tu shes pas brtan pa rab tu mkhas shing pha rol phyin gyur pas/_/mkhas pa dag ni dag cing sbyangs pa'i byang chub phyag rgya snang ba'i ye shes kyis/_/'gro ba dag dang [{]myi [,]mi [}]mtshungs zhum [{]myed [,]med [}]khyu mchog lhag pa'i bsam pa ri bo 'dra/_/ye shes rgya mtsho 'dra dang 
[\[272a.3\]]yon tan [{]myi [,]mi [}]zad don du spyod pa chen po spyod/_/de dag rin chen 'dra ba'i gnas la rdo rje lta bu'i go cha bgos pas gnas/_/shin tu rgya chen rtsom la rab tu mkhas shing 'gro ba kun na thub [{]myed [,]med [}]pa/_/byang chub mchog tu lung bstan skyon [{]myed [,]med [}]khong du chud pa'i rgya chen 
[\[272a.4\]]sems la gnas/_/sangs rgyas gter mchog mi zad thob nas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 'tshang rgya'o/_/rtag tu kun rdzob shes pas dbang 'gyur rnam par 'phrul pa rnams la mkhas pas na/_/zhing rnams kun dang chos kyi yul la rnam par 'phrul 
[\[272a.5\]]ba'i ye shes rgyud la gnas/_/lus dang smon lam spyod yul ye shes rnam par 'phrul pa la yang de bzhin te/_/'gro ba dag la rnam par rtse ba bye ba khrag khrig du ma mang po ston/_/mngon par shes pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs kyis rnam par 'phrul zhing nges par rnam par 
[\[272a.6\]]rtse/_/khyu mchog de dag sangs rgyas yul la stobs thob 'gro ba kun gyis thub pa [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/mkhas pa 'jigs pa [{]myed [,]med [}]dang bag tsha [{]myed [,]med [}]dang ma 'dres rnams kyis shin tu brgyan/_/sangs rgyas sras dag las rnams thams cad byed cing shin tu mdzes pa'i lus dang 
[\[272a.7\]]ldan/_/de dag lus kyi las kyi dngos po shin tu rgya che dag kyang dag cing dge_/de yang 'byin pa'i tshe na rgyal bas byin gyis brlabs shing las ni mchog kyang byed/_/dam pa de dag sems bskyed gcig la sems rgyas byed pa'i sems kyang rab tu ston/_/rgyal 
[\[272b\]]
[\[272b.1\]]ba'i dbang po thob pas rtag tu dbang po'i ting 'dzin dbang la rab tu gnas/_/bsam pa dag cing g.yo sgyu rnams dang bral te lhag pa'i bsam pa mngon sum gyur/_/mos pa'i 'jug pa rnam pa sna tshogs tha dad 'gro ba dag la rab tu ston/_/bag chags thams 
[\[272b.2\]]cad spangs nas spyod pa dam pa rab tu 'dzin/_/nan tan bya ba grub pa'i ye shes mkhas pas thams cad mkhyen pa 'tshang yang rgya/_/bsam pa rnam par spangs nas de dag skyon med gnas pa zhi ba'i mchog kyang thob/_/mkhas pa 'di lam shin tu bslabs pa de dag ming ni 
[\[272b.3\]]yon tan sgo nas grags/_/mtha' yas sems kyi yul ldan gnas pa [{]myed [,]med [}]par lam rnams yang du sgom pa byed/_/ye shes tshul la gnas shing 'gro ba rnams la lam gyi rgyan rnams shin tu ston/_/spyod pa lag pa lta bu dga' dang ldan pa lto yin snying po ye shes mchog_/
[\[272b.4\]]snying ni rdo rje lta bu snying rje'i go bgos ye shes gdab pa'i mtshon cha yin/_/shes rab mgo ste chos rtogs [{]myig [,]mig [}]yin byang chub spyod pa yang ni rna ba yin/_/tshul khrims dag cing nyon mongs [{]myed [,]med [}]la gtsang ste mun pa zhi byed sna yin te/_/spobs pa lce yin lus ni rig pa yin 
[\[272b.5\]]te sems ni ye shes dam pa yin/_/spyod pa rgyu ba yin te byang chub shing gnas seng ge'i khri ni 'dug pa yin/_/tshangs pa'i lam rnams dag ni lam yin stong pa chags [{]myed [,]med [}]dam pa spyod yul lo/_/gang gi de dag snang bar 'gyur ba'i snang ba bde bar gshegs kyi ye 
[\[272b.6\]]shes yul/_/sems can spyod la rnam par lta ba ye shes mchog ste rnam pa sna tshogs bsgyings/_/sems can dben zhing chags dang bral ba'i sbyin pa bsnyems pa [{]myed [,]med [}]pa'i tshul khrims dang /_/bzod pa mi 'khrug brtson 'grus [{]myi [,]mi [}]ldog dbang bsgyur ba ni bsam gtan 
[\[272b.7\]]dam pa yin/_/rgyu ba [{]myed [,]med [}]cing nam mkha' 'dra ba shes rab byams pa sems can rnam par 'dren/_/skyo med snying rje rnam dag chos ni dga' ba nyon mongs gnas las btang yang snyoms/_/don ni zhi ba chos kyi yul la nye bar 'gro ba bsod nams 'gro la bsngos/_/ye shes 
[\[273a\]]
[\[273a.1\]]tshogs ni ral gyi rnon po lta bu snang ba chen po rig pa ste/_/'gro ba rnams la thos pa ston pas mi ngoms bskal par 'dren kyang 'jigs pa [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/nan tan byed la gnas te bdud rnams btul nas bdud kyi lam yang rab tu bcad/_/rgyal ba 
[\[273a.2\]]rnams kyi 'phrin las gang yin ye shes spyod dang lhag pa'i bsam pas 'dzin/_/nga rgyal las rnams rnam par spangs te rtag tu ye shes kyi 'ang bya ba byed/_/bdud rnams byin gyi rlabs bcom mkhas pa de dag sangs rgyas byin gyi rlabs kyis 'phags/_/chos kyi byin 
[\[273a.3\]]gyi rlabs thob blo gros chen po 'chang ba lha yi gnas su 'dod/_/lha rnams byas te 'chi 'phos song nas mchog gi mngal ni shin tu phra bar 'gro/_/skye ba mchog ni nye bar bstan nas mchog gi gom pa bdun ni 'dor bar byed/_/mkhas pa de dag sems can kun la 
[\[273a.4\]]bzo dang spyod dang btsun mo'i 'khor kyang ston/_/chags pa [{]myed [,]med [}]par mngon par byung nas byang chub shing drung chos kyi rgyan kyang byed/_/stong pa'i bstan las 'od zer dra ba yangs pa bkye zhing yang dag bskul ba byed/_/bdud rnams btul te byang chub go 'phang sangs rgyas 
[\[273a.5\]]chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor/_/sangs rgyas sa yang rab tu bstan nas 'dren pa de dag mtshungs pa [{]myed [,]med [}]par bzhud/_/gang gis mkhas pa bskal pa bye bar rnam par rtse zhing yang dag byung gyur nas/_/sems can bye ba khrag khrig stong phrag du ma sangs rgyas yon 
[\[273a.6\]]tan rab 'god kyang /_/rnam pa kun tu sems can [{]myi [,]mi [}]gnas chos lam yin spyod lam yin pa'i/_/de dag gi ni spyod pa 'di 'dra mtha' yas shin tu rgya chen mdor bstan to/_/de 'dra'i spyod dang ldan pa'i ye shes khyu mchog rdzu 'phrul dag gis rnam par rtse/_/zhing 
[\[273a.7\]]rnams bye ba stong yang skra rtse gcig la bskal pa khrag khrig brgyar 'dzin te/_/lag tu khyer te zhing rnams bye bar 'gro yang lus la ngal dub gang yang [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/mkhas pas khyer nas ji lta'i gnas su bkod kyang 'gro ba dag la gnod pa [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/'jig rten mkhyen pa'i zhing 
[\[273b\]]
[\[273b.1\]]gi rgyan rnams ji snyed rnam pa tha dad bstan pa dag_/kun kyang ba spu gcig gi bu gar khyu mchog de dag yang dag rab tu ston/_/mkhas pa ba spu'i bu gar rgya mtsho'i chu rnams ma lus khong chud rab ston kyang /_/'phel zhing 'grib [{]myed [,]med [}]'gro ba dag la gnod par 'gyur [{]myed [,]med [}]bya 
[\[273b.2\]]ba de yang ston/_/khor yug khrag khrig shin tu phra ba'i rdul byas lag gis bsdus khyer zhing /_/zhing rnams khrag khrig bye ma snyed du 'dor byed gcig tu gnyis kyang gzhog mi byed/_/zhing rnams de dag rdul du byas te de bzhin gzhan yang rnam par 'jig byed 
[\[273b.3\]]de/_/de 'dra'i rjes su dpag pa zad par gyur kyang 'jug pa de dag mi zad do/_/'jig rten kun gyi nyi ma zla ba skar ma gang dag stong rnams ji snyed dang /_/gang yang nor bu'i 'od dang [{]mye [,]me [}]yi 'od dang gang na gang rnams lha dbang 'od/_/mang po de dag rab tu 'das 
[\[273b.4\]]pa'i 'od zer khrag khrig spu gcig khung nas 'gyed/_/'jig rten kun gyi ngan song sdug bsngal zhi bar byed cing chos kyi dam pa ston/_/'gro ba ma lus sgra yi rnam pa sna tshogs skad rnams thams cad ji snyed pa/_/khyu mchog de dag tshig gcig rab tu 
[\[273b.5\]]brjod pas sgra rnams thams cad tshang bar brjod/_/srog chags bye ba rnams kyis yid du 'ong ba'i sgra thos dga' zhing mgu bar 'gyur/_/rgyal bas bstan pa'i chos kyi rnam pa ji snyed thams cad de las thos par 'gyur/_/gang yang ji snyed 'das pa'i 
[\[273b.6\]]bskal pa yod pa de dag ma 'ongs bskal par ston/_/gang yang ma 'ongs pa dang da ltar byung ba ji snyed de dag 'das par ston/_/khyu mchog dag ni zhing rnams 'tshig dang gnas pa rnam pa tha dad ston par byed/_/'gro ba ji snyed thams cad mkhas pa de dag skra rtse gcig 
[\[273b.7\]]la rab tu 'god/_/'das dang ma byon pa dang phyogs bcu kun na bzhugs pa'i rgyal ba ji snyed pa/_/de dag thams cad rang gi lus las ma nor dbang bsgyur rnam par 'phrul ba ston/_/sprul pa'i byin gyi rlabs la shes pa'i mkhas pa de dag 'gro ba ji snyed sems/_/
[\[274a\]]
[\[274a.1\]]de snyed lus rnams rdul dang bral ba ston kyang lus la gnas pa gang yang [{]myed[,]med[}]/_/lam rgyud drug po gang rnams ji snyed yod pa'i 'gro ba kun gyi lus bsdus shing /_/brgya byin tshangs pa 'jig rten mgon po rnams dang de bzhin lha dang 
[\[274a.2\]{]myi [,]mi [}]yi rnams/_/gang yang nyan thos rang rgyal rnams la dad cing sangs rgyas sku la mos pa la/_/sangs rgyas byang chub 'grub pa'i spyod pa dag dang thams cad mkhyen pa'i spyod pa ston/_/'gro ba kun gyi 'du shes dra ba rab dang 'bring dang tha mar 'jug pa rnams/_/
[\[274a.3\]]thams cad shes shing byin rlob byang chub 'tshang rgya zhing rnams kun kyang rab tu ston/_/'du shes dra ba rnams sgoms blo ldan 'jig rten 'du shes rnams la dbang bsgyur ba/_/bya ba dang ni sbyor ba ji snyed pa dang dkon mchog byang chub spyod pa ston/_/de dag de dang 
[\[274a.4\]]rnam par 'phrul pa shin tu rgya chen dam pa gzhan yang ston/_/de dag gang la 'jig rten sa [{]myin [,]min [}]pa dang yul [{]myin [,]min [}]de dag bya ba ston/_/ston kyang lta ba lhur len ma yin gzhan yang phyir zhing gong du ston pa byed/_/de dag sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji bzhin spyod pa ji bzhin yang dag 
[\[274a.5\]]'jug_/lus ni nam mkha' lta bu dag dang yid la chags [{]myed [,]med [}]'jig rten gsum gyi gtso/_tshul khrims spos kyi byug pa rab tu zhi ba gos yin yon tan sna tshogs rgyan/_/chos kyi go 'phang skyon [{]myed [,]med [}]thob cing thams cad mkhyen pa yid bzhin nor bu ste/_/bde gshegs kun gyi 
[\[274a.6\]]ye shes dbang bskur thams cad mkhyen pa'i rgyal po'i thabs la gnas/_/pha rol phyin pa rtag 'byung 'khor lo yin te glang po mngon par shes pa mchog_/ye shes rdzu 'phrul rta ste chags pa [{]myed [,]med [}]'gro shes rab nor bu dam pa yin/_/spyod pa btsun mo bzang mo bsdu ba rnam pa bzhi po khyim bdag dam pa'o/_/
[\[274a.7\]]thabs la shin tu mkhas pa blon po yin te mkhas pa de dag 'khor los sgyur/_/stong pa khyim ste ting 'dzin grong khyer mchog yin byams dang snying rje'i go ca'o/_/shes rab ral gyi yin te dran pa gzhu mdzes dbang po rno ba mda' yang yin/_/'jig rten mkhyen pa rnams kyi byin gyi rlabs ni 
[\[274b\]]
[\[274b.1\]]gdugs te ye shes rgyal mtshan bsgreng /_/de dang de dag bzod pa'i stobs kyis bdud kyi dpung dang sde rnams 'dus pa 'joms/_/gzungs ni sa ste byang chub spyod pa chu klung ye shes lu ma shes rab shing /_/me tog byang chub yan lag ting nge 'dzin ni rta ste rdzu 'phrul 
[\[274b.2\]]rnam par brgyan/_/stong pa rnam thar brgyad pa rdzing bu yin te rnam par dpyod pa bzang mo btsun mo yin/_/thar ba bdud rtsi bza' ba yin te chos ni btung ba theg pa gsum gyis rtse/_/de dag spyod pa de 'dra yid 'ong khyad par 'phags te de bas gong du mang /_/gang gis 
[\[274b.3\]]bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig stong du skyo ba yongs su [{]myi [,]mi [}]skyo bar/_/ston pa mchod cing rnam dag zhing la brtson pas 'gro ba ye shes mchog la 'god/_/kha na ma tho kun [{]myed [,]med [}]ye shes rab gnas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rgyas byed/_/zhing rnams rdul gyi 'brur blags re re nas kyang 
[\[274b.4\]]bgrang zhing brtsi bar nus gyur la/_/bar snang mkha' chen 'di yang skra yis gzhal te zad par bya bar nus srid de/_/skad cig gcig la 'gro ba rnams kyi sems ni bgrang tshul shes par nus srid kyi/_/sangs rgyas sras rnams spyod pa dag ni bskal pa brgyar 
[\[274b.5\]]brjod zad par [{]myi [,]mi [}]nus so/_/gang zhig yon tan rnam dag 'di dag 'thob par 'dod cing gong du gzhan 'dod dang /_/gang zhig sdug bsngal rnams las 'gro bar thar byas bde la 'god pa dang /_/gang zhig bde bar gshegs pa'i sku gsung thugs dang mnyam du 
[\[274b.6\]]'gyur bar 'dod pa dag_/des ni rdo rje lta bur brtan pa'i sems su gyis te yon tan bslab par byos/_/shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i le'u ste/_bzhi bcu rtsa bzhi pa'o//_//
